- **gestures-and-keys**: **Gestures aim by ref.** `click`/`tap`/`drag`/`scroll` take `{"ref":"e12"}` (or a native screen's `{"ref":"n4"}`) in place of `x`,`y` and land on that element's centre. A rect's `x,y` is its corner, which is padding, and a click there reports `no change`. `{"text":"…"}` aims at that text on screen: in the editor its words, on a phone the one row or words that show it (text two places show is refus ([futo-org/futo-notes](https://github.com/futo-org/futo-notes/blob/HEAD/.claude/skills/puppet/references/commands/gestures-and-keys.md) · ★54)

- **git-commit-generator**: Generate professional, detailed git commit messages from staged changes or diffs. Use this skill whenever the user wants to create a commit message, generate a git commit, summarize changes for a commit, or asks anything like "write a commit for my changes", "generate a commit message", "what should my commit say", "اعمل commit", "اكتب commit message". The skill produces structured commit messages that explain what changed, why, and the impact on the project — with zero mention of AI tools or agents.
